                                LETTER OF PROMULGATION

1. NWP 22(A), DOCTRINE FOR AMPHIBIOUS OPERATIONS (U), is an
Unclassified Nonregistered publication. Administrative procedures con-
cerning the handling of this publication by the holders are contained in the
effective edition of NWP 0, Guide and Master Index to the NWP’s.
2. NWP 22(A) is EFFECTIVE upon receipt. R supersedes NWP 22,
AMPHIBIOUS OPERATIONS (U), which is to be destroyed by burning with-
out report.
3. Within the Marine Corps Publication System, this publication is assigned
the short title LFM-01, an Unclassified Nonregistered publication to be
effective upon receipt.
4. The FORWARD to this publication sets forth its source and status.
5. There is no objection to making extracts from these publications.

and poor cross-country mobility. These limitations      operating techniques and procedures, and maintenance
require careful and detailed consideration during       of equipment. (See Naval Arctic Operations Hand-

ious operations, to include provision for:              ship (H.O. No. 551); Cold Weather Operating Proce-
